# Attribution - recent Libya texts used for Rico's training data

Collected on **2026-10-02** via the public MediaWiki APIs by `ml/collect_recent.py`; only pages whose latest revision is dated **2026-04-01** or later were kept (the *current* text of each page, plain-text extract).

## Licenses

* **Arabic Wikipedia** (https://ar.wikipedia.org): Creative Commons Attribution-Share Alike 4.0 - https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/deed.ar
* **Arabic Wikinews** (https://ar.wikinews.org): Creative Commons Attribution-Share Alike 4.0 - https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/deed.ar
* **English Wikipedia** (https://en.wikipedia.org): Creative Commons Attribution-Share Alike 4.0 - https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/deed.en

Text is available under the licenses above (see each site's *Terms of Use*). Authors are credited through the page-history links below. Question/answer pairs derived from these pages (records with `"source": "recent_qa"` in `ml/data/rico_sft.jsonl`) are adaptations: keep this attribution file with the data and, for CC BY-SA sources, share the data under the same license. These are summaries of third-party community text; they can be wrong or outdated - Rico must never present them as verified news.
